Abstract: Human Activity Recognition (HAR) using WiFi signals has emerged as a transformative technology for smart homes, healthcare monitoring, security systems, and ambient assisted living. Unlike traditional camera-based systems that raise significant privacy concerns and fail in low-light conditions, or wearable sensors that require user compliance, WiFi-based HAR is non-intrusive, privacy-preserving, cost-effective, and works seamlessly in any lighting condition. This paper presents a comprehensive approach to recognize three distinct human activities: "No Presence" (empty room), "Walking", and "Walking + Arm-waving" using the Wallhack1.8k WiFi spectrogram dataset. We propose three key improvements to address the main challenges in WiFi-based HAR. First, to address high performance variance, we implement ensemble learning with five different CNN architectures (Deep CNN, Wide CNN, MobileNetV2, ResNet50V2, and EfficientNetB0). Second, to address the small dataset size limitation, we apply aggressive data augmentation techniques including time-warping, frequency masking, and noise addition. Third, to evaluate real-world generalization capability, we perform cross-scenario evaluation (training on Line-of-Sight and testing on Non-Line-of-Sight) and cross-antenna evaluation (training on Biquad antenna and testing on PIFA antenna). Our ensemble model achieved a test accuracy of 94.87% on the LOS scenario with Biquad antenna, outperforming the best individual model by 0.66%. Data augmentation improved Random Forest performance from 60% to 95%. Cross-scenario evaluation showed minimal accuracy drops of only 1.37% and 2.07%, demonstrating strong generalization capabilities. The results indicate that the proposed approach is robust, reliable, and suitable for real-world deployment in diverse environments with different hardware configurations.

</details>


### [86] [Inducing Reasoning Primitives from Agent Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02994)
*Zhihan Lei,Jiarui Yan,Joshua Momo,William W. Cohen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出推理原语归纳方法：从ReAct智能体的成功轨迹中挖掘高频推理模式，聚类后构建带自然语言文档字符串的类型化伪工具库，在推理时由标准ReAct循环组合调用。该方法使得归纳出的工具库性能超越生成轨迹的原始智能体，在多个推理基准上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ReAct风格的LLM智能体在不同问题间反复"重新发现"相同的推理套路，但这些有价值的推理模式仅存在于瞬态草稿中，未能被复用。作者旨在将这些隐式的推理知识显式化、结构化，形成可复用的原语库。

Method: 提出推理原语归纳（Reasoning Primitive Induction），一种单遍方法：(1) 挖掘成功的ReAct轨迹；(2) 对反复出现的推理动作进行聚类；(3) 将高频推理动作转化为类型化伪工具（pseudo-tools），每个伪工具配有自然语言docstring供LLM调用时解释；(4) 测试时由标准ReAct循环组合这些原语。

Result: 归纳出的工具库性能大幅超越生成轨迹的原始智能体：RuleArena NBA提升44个百分点（30→74），MuSR团队分配提升30个百分点（38→68），NatPlan会议规划提升22个百分点（7→29）。在涵盖叙事演绎、规则应用和约束满足规划的五个可比子任务上，单一固定配置全面超越零样本思维链，匹配或超越专家编写的分解方案，并以更低平均推理成本超越AWM。

Conclusion: 从智能体自身成功轨迹中归纳推理原语是一种高效的知识蒸馏与复用范式，能够在不增加推理成本的前提下显著提升推理性能，甚至超越生成训练数据的原始智能体。

Abstract: ReAct-style LLM agents often rediscover the same reasoning routines across problems, yet leave those routines trapped in transient scratchpads. We introduce Reasoning Primitive Induction, a single-pass method that mines successful ReAct traces, clusters recurrent reasoning moves, and converts the most frequent moves into a compact library of typed pseudo-tools. Each pseudo-tool is specified by a natural-language docstring interpreted by an LLM at invocation time, and a standard ReAct loop composes these primitives at test time. The central result is that induced libraries outperform the very agent that generated their traces: by +44pp on RuleArena NBA (30 -> 74), +30pp on MuSR team allocation (38 -> 68), and +22pp on NatPlan meeting planning (7 -> 29). Across five comparable subtasks spanning narrative deduction, rule application, and constraint-satisfaction planning, a single fixed configuration improves over zero-shot Chain-of-Thought on every subtask, matches or surpasses expert-authored decompositions, and outperforms AWM at lower average inference cost.

</details>


### [87] [AUDITFLOW: Executable Symbolic Environments for Structured Financial Reporting Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03031)
*Yan Wang,Xuguang Ai,Jaisal Patel,Xueqing Peng,Fengran Mo,Yupeng Cao,Haohang Li,Mingyu Cao,Lingfei Qian,Víctor Gutiérrez-Basulto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AuditFlow 是一个图基多智能体审计框架，将自适应搜索与确定性验证分离，通过在符号化环境中结合 XBRL 分类法和申报图进行结构化财务审计验证，在 FinMR 数据集上取得 82.09% 的联合审计准确率，超出最强基线 14.93 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化财务审计验证对语言模型代理而言极具挑战，因为审计正确性依赖结构化证据（如分类法概念链接、计算/维度关系遍历、期望值重算）而非纯文本推理。现有方法缺乏将符号化验证与模型搜索有效结合的机制。

Method: 提出 AuditFlow，一个图基多智能体框架：(1) 构建符号化环境，包含静态 US-GAAP 分类法图和动态 XBRL 申报图，并通过类型化工具暴露事实检索、分类法遍历、数值校验和规则评估功能；(2) 两名初级审计员分别从监管视角和证据视角审查案例；(3) 高级审计员负责裁决分歧并可要求进一步调查；(4) 通过证据聚合融合最终报告，输出审计结论、期望值、证据链和可信度评分。

Result: 在 FinAuditing 衍生的 FinMR 样本上，AuditFlow 在 GPT-5.5 下达到 82.09% 的联合审计准确率，超出最强基线 14.93 个百分点。消融实验中移除确定性检查后准确率骤降至 17.91%，证明符号化环境承担了模型无法可靠替代的验证步骤。

Conclusion: 语言模型不应单独承担结构化审计中的验证任务；将确定性符号验证与模型的自适应搜索解耦，是提升审计准确性和可信度的有效路径。AuditFlow 的架构设计验证了这一思路的可行性。

Abstract: Structured financial audit verification is difficult for language-model agents because correctness depends on structured evidence rather than text alone. A model must link reported facts to taxonomy concepts, traverse calculation or dimensional relations, and recompute expected values before applying an audit rule. We propose AuditFlow, a graph-grounded multi-agent framework that separates adaptive search from deterministic verification. AuditFlow builds a symbolic environment from a static US-GAAP taxonomy graph and a dynamic XBRL filing graph, and exposes it through typed tools for fact retrieval, taxonomy traversal, numerical checking, and rule evaluation. Two junior auditors inspect each case from regulatory and evidentiary views, while a senior auditor resolves disagreements and can request further investigation. The final reports are fused through evidential aggregation to produce an audit verdict, expected value, evidence trail, and trustworthiness score. On a FinAuditing-derived FinMR sample, AuditFlow reaches 82.09% joint audit accuracy under GPT-5.5, outperforming the strongest baseline by 14.93 points. Removing deterministic checks drops accuracy to 17.91%, showing that the symbolic environment performs the verification step that the model cannot reliably replace.

</details>


### [88] [TriEval: A Resource-Efficient Pipeline for LLM Bias, Toxicity, and Truthfulness Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03036)
*Akshatha Srikantha,Manpreet Singh,Yash Jajoo,Shyamal Lakhanpal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TriEval is a lightweight, multi-parameter evaluation pipeline for LLMs that simultaneously assesses bias, toxicity, and truthfulness, running on standard laptops without GPU clusters. Tested on four models, it reveals notable gaps between open-source and closed-source LLMs.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs已从基础聊天机器人演变为广泛应用于医疗、教育和政府等领域的AI基础设施，部署后常出现输出不一致和幻觉等问题。现有评估工具大多一次仅测试单一参数，或需要海量计算资源，限制了多数研究者的使用。TriEval旨在解决这些痛点。

Method: TriEval构建了一个多参数并行评估流水线，可同时评估LLM输出的偏见（bias）、毒性（toxicity）和真实性（truthfulness），兼容开源与闭源模型，且仅需标准笔记本电脑即可运行，无需GPU集群。

Result: 在Llama 3 8B、Mistral 7B、Gemma 2 9B和Claude Haiku四个模型上的测试表明，开源与闭源模型在毒性和真实性方面存在明显差异。

Conclusion: TriEval以开源形式发布，为计算资源有限的研究者提供了一种可广泛访问的LLM安全性与公平性评估工具，推动了对LLM多维性能的系统化、低门槛评估。

Abstract: LLMs have evolved from basic chatbots to the backbone of the AI ecosystem, now widely used in healthcare, schools, and government services. The domain-wide adoption of LLMs necessitates continuous evaluation to ensure their safety and fairness. Common issues encountered after deploying LLMs include inconsistent outputs and hallucinations of incorrect information. Although numerous LLM evaluation tools exist, most are limited to testing a single parameter at a time or require massive computational resources that are not accessible to most researchers. TriEval addresses these challenges by evaluating LLM outputs across multiple parameters, including bias, toxicity, and truthfulness together, while minimizing computing resources. The pipeline is compatible with both open- and closed-source models and runs on a standard laptop without a GPU cluster. TriEval has been tested on four models: Llama 3 8B, Mistral 7B, Gemma 2 9B, and Claude Haiku. The results show clear differences between open-source and closed-source models, especially in terms of toxicity and truthfulness. TriEval is being released as open source to enable broader access for researchers with limited computational resources.

</details>


### [89] [RelGT-AC: A Relational Graph Transformer for Autocomplete Tasks in Relational Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03040)
*Phillip Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RelGT-AC模型，通过列掩码策略、统一任务头和TF-IDF文本编码器三项改进扩展RelGT架构，在RelBench v2的3个数据集7个任务上全面超越GraphSAGE基线，尤其在文本密集型任务上AUROC提升达10个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关系数据库中的预测性机器学习面临多表、异构和时序结构的挑战。RelBench v2引入的自动补全任务（autocomplete）具有实际应用价值，但现有方法存在三个不足：容易学到 trivial 解、缺乏统一的多任务支持、以及丢弃了自由文本列中的词汇信号。

Method: 以RelGT架构为基础，提出三项扩展：（1）列掩码策略——在子图编码阶段掩码目标列，防止模型走捷径；（2）统一任务头——单一模型同时支持二分类、多分类和回归三种自动补全任务；（3）TF-IDF文本编码器——自动检测并编码自由文本列，恢复被类别编码器丢弃的词汇信号。

Result: 在RelBench v2的rel-trial、rel-f1、rel-stack三个数据集共7个任务上，RelGT-AC在所有3个回归自动补全任务上超越GraphSAGE基线；借助TF-IDF编码器，在文本密集的eligibility任务上AUROC提升最高达10个点。

Conclusion: RelGT-AC通过三项针对性改进有效提升了关系深度学习在自动补全任务上的表现，尤其验证了保留文本列的词汇信号对预测性能的重要性。

Abstract: Relational databases underpin modern enterprise, scientific, and healthcare systems, yet predictive machine learning on such data remains challenging due to their multi-table, heterogeneous, and temporal structure. Relational Deep Learning (RDL) addresses this by representing databases as heterogeneous graphs and applying graph neural networks (GNNs) directly. RelBench v2 recently introduced autocomplete tasks -- a practically motivated task type where the goal is to predict an existing column value from relational context, analogous to an intelligent form-filling assistant. We propose RelGT-AC (Relational Graph Transformer for Autocomplete), extending the RelGT architecture with three targeted contributions: (1) a column masking strategy that prevents trivial solutions by masking the target column during subgraph encoding; (2) a unified task head supporting binary classification, multiclass classification, and regression autocomplete tasks within a single model; and (3) a TF-IDF text encoder that automatically detects and encodes free-text columns, recovering strong lexical signal that categorical encoders discard. Across 7 tasks spanning 3 RelBench v2 datasets (rel-trial, rel-f1, rel-stack), RelGT-AC outperforms the GraphSAGE baseline on all 3 regression autocomplete tasks and achieves up to +10 AUROC points on text-heavy eligibility tasks via the TF-IDF encoder.

</details>


### [90] [Do Real-World Datasets Contain Natural Experiments? An Empirical Study Using Causal Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03251)
*Gautam Gare,John Galeotti,Michael Mozer,Deva Ramanan,Nan Rosemary Ke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出真实世界数据集中存在"自然实验"（如COVID-19疫情对特定子群体的影响），并利用因果发现方法检测这些自然实验。研究表明，将数据视为干预性而非观察性处理可提升下游模型性能，初步验证了这一假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中存在类似自然实验的事件（如疫情、政策变化），它们仅影响部分个体或群体，构成隐式干预。作者希望探究：真实世界数据集中是否普遍存在这种自然实验？若存在，应如何识别并加以利用？

Method: 采用因果发现算法恢复数据背后的因果图，基于因果链接进行特征选择，以此检测自然实验。若将数据作为干预性数据处理的性能优于作为观察性数据处理，则判定该数据集包含自然实验。先通过合成图模拟含与不含自然实验的数据集进行验证，再在大量真实数据集上进行系统实证评估。

Result: 合成数据验证了检测方法的有效性；真实世界数据集的系统评估表明，真实数据中确实存在自然实验，且利用因果推断方法加以利用可有效提升模型性能。

Conclusion: 真实世界数据集包含自然实验，可通过因果推断加以利用以改善模型性能。该工作是对这一领域的初步探索，范围有限，但开启了新的研究方向。

Abstract: In nature, events that affect some individuals or groups but not others constitute an implicit intervention and are known as natural experiments. For example, the COVID-19 pandemic was an intervention by the coronavirus on the sub-population infected with COVID. We ask, do natural experiments occur in existing real-world datasets? If yes, how should we treat them? To detect natural experiments in data, we use causal discovery to recover the underlying causal graph and perform feature selection based on causal links. If downstream performance improves by treating the data as interventional rather than observational, we argue that this suggests the dataset contains natural experiments. We first validate this hypothesis by simulating datasets with and without natural experiments using synthetic graphs. We then perform a systematic empirical evaluation on a large suite of real-world datasets. Our results indicate that real-world datasets do contain natural experiments and we can take advantage of those natural experiments to improve model performance using causal inference. Our work represents the initial foray into this area, offering a preliminary exploration within a limited scope.

</details>


### [91] [ToolGate: Token-Efficient Pre-Call Control for Tool-Augmented Vision-Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03054)
*Anjie Liu,Yan Song,Zhixun Chen,Ziqin Gong,Zhongwei Yu,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究工具增强型视觉语言智能体的"预调用控制"问题，提出轻量级外部控制器ToolGate，用于预测感知工具调用应执行还是跳过，从而在保持准确率的同时将token成本降至基准的64-69%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在ReAct风格的VLM智能体中，每次执行OCR、检测、分割等感知工具调用成本高昂且并非总是必要。基线智能体在局部选择性上表现差：有益调用（11.8%）和有害调用（9.9%）发生率相近，而大多数调用不会改变即时强制回答预测。因此需要一种机制来判断工具调用是否值得执行。

Method: 提出ToolGate——一个轻量级外部控制器，利用轨迹文本和简单结构特征预测每个感知工具调用的"执行/跳过"二元决策。该控制器独立于VLM主体，在工具调用执行前进行拦截判断，避免不必要的工具输出进入上下文窗口。

Result: 在五个基准测试上、两个Qwen3-VL骨干模型下，ToolGate在跨域设置中将token成本降低至无限制ReAct基线的64-69%，同时保持平均准确率；在Qwen3-VL-30B的匹配域轨迹训练下，平均准确率进一步提升1.65个百分点。

Conclusion: 工具增强型VLM智能体不仅受益于更好的感知工具，还受益于对"何时值得为工具输出付出代价"的显式控制。预调用控制是提升智能体效率与性能的有效方向。

Abstract: Tool-augmented vision-language agents can acquire external perceptual evidence through OCR, detection, segmentation, and other tools, but executing every proposed tool call is costly and sometimes unnecessary. We study the pre-call control problem: after a ReAct-style VLM agent proposes a perceptual tool call, should the call be executed, or skipped before its output enters the context? Across five benchmarks, we find that the baseline agent exhibits poor local selectivity: helpful and harmful calls occur at similar rates (11.8% vs. 9.9%), while most calls do not change the immediate forced-answer prediction. We introduce ToolGate, a lightweight external controller that predicts execute/skip decisions from trajectory text and simple structural features. Across two Qwen3-VL backbones, ToolGate reduces token cost to 64-69% of the unrestricted ReAct baseline while preserving average accuracy in cross-domain settings. With matched-domain trajectory training on Qwen3-VL-30B, it further improves average accuracy by 1.65 points. These results show that tool-augmented VLM agents benefit not only from better perceptual tools, but also from explicit control over when tool outputs are worth paying for.

</details>


### [92] [SkillDAG: Self-Evolving Typed Skill Graphs for LLM Skill Selection at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03056)
*Tong Bai,Zhenglin Wan,Pengfei Zhou,Xingrui Yu,Wangbo Zhao,Yang You,Ivor W. Tsang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillDAG 将技能间关系建模为有向图，通过推理时可调用的结构化检索接口，让 LLM Agent 在执行过程中动态查询和演化技能关系，在 ALFWorld 和 SkillsBench 上显著超越最强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型技能库的选取不是简单的相似度匹配问题，技能之间存在依赖、冲突、特化、重复等复杂结构关系，这些关系对全量枚举和嵌入相似度方法均不可见，需要一种能建模并利用这些关系的新方法。

Method: SkillDAG 将技能间关系建模为类型化有向图（typed directed graph），作为推理时可调用的结构化检索接口暴露给 LLM Agent。每次检索返回向量匹配、类型边邻居和冲突信号；采用 propose-then-commit 协议让 Agent 在执行中注册经验支撑的边，使图谱跨 episode 持续积累结构。

Result: 在 ALFWorld 和 SkillsBench 上使用 MiniMax-M2.7，SkillDAG 达到 67.1% 成功率和 27.3% 奖励，比最强 Graph-of-Skills 基线分别提升 +12.8 和 +8.6 个百分点；优势可迁移至 gpt-5.2-codex。SkillsBench Ret@K 从 65.5 提升到 78.2。技能池增长 10 倍时候选排序保持鲁棒，而固定 seeding-diffusion pipeline 会退化；集合单调在线编辑在不驱逐已有命中项的情况下扩大了 ground-truth 召回。

Conclusion: 技能关系的有向图建模与动态演化机制是提升 LLM Agent 在大型技能库中高效选取子技能的关键，SkillDAG 的性能增益可归因于鲁棒的候选排序和单调递增的在线图编辑这两个可分离机制。

Abstract: As LLM agents adopt large skill libraries, selecting the right subset becomes a structural problem rather than a similarity-matching one: skills depend on, conflict with, specialize, or duplicate one another, a structure invisible to both full enumeration and embedding similarity. We present SkillDAG, which models inter-skill relationships as a typed directed graph and exposes it to an LLM agent as an inference-time, agent-callable structural retrieval interface, queried and evolved during execution rather than baked into a fixed retrieval pipeline: each search returns vector matches, typed-edge neighbors, and conflict signals, and a propose-then-commit protocol lets the agent register execution-backed edges so the graph accumulates structure across episodes. On ALFWorld and SkillsBench with MiniMax-M2.7, SkillDAG reaches 67.1% success and 27.3% reward, exceeding the strongest reported Graph-of-Skills baseline by +12.8 and +8.6 points; the advantage ports to gpt-5.2-codex, and intrinsic SkillsBench Ret@K rises from 65.5 to 78.2 under matched queries. These gains trace to isolable mechanisms: candidate ranking that stays robust as the pool grows 10x where a fixed seeding-diffusion pipeline degrades, and set-monotone online edits that enlarge ground-truth recall without evicting prior hits.

</details>


### [93] [CORE: Conflict-Oriented Reasoning for General Multimodal Manipulation Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03066)
*Jinjie Shen,Yaxiong Wang,Yujiao Wu,Lechao Cheng,Tianrui Hui,Nan Pu,Zhihui Li,Zhun Zhong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CORE框架通过赋予多模态大语言模型显式的冲突捕捉能力，构建了冲突归因语料库(CAC)并进行冲突导向的表示增强与推理，在伪造新闻检测中实现了对未知操纵类型的强泛化能力，仅需少量样本甚至零样本即可适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态假新闻检测方法严重依赖特定操纵类型的模型和大规模标注数据，对新兴操纵类型泛化能力差。作者观察到，操纵性错误信息的本质在于其内在冲突——即跨模态之间或与常识世界知识之间的语义或物理不一致性。

Method: 提出CORE（Conflict-Oriented REasoning）框架：(1) 首先构建冲突归因语料库(CAC)，对冲突因素和来源进行细粒度标注；(2) 基于CAC进行冲突导向的表示增强和推理训练，使MLLM获得显式的冲突捕捉能力；(3) 通过该框架实现对新操纵类型的快速适应，支持少样本和零样本场景。

Result: 大量实验表明，CORE超越了现有最先进模型，在应对未见过的操纵类型时表现出鲁棒且可泛化的检测性能。

Conclusion: 通过聚焦于多模态信息中的内在冲突而非特定操纵痕迹，CORE为假新闻检测提供了一种更具泛化性和实用性的新范式，有效应对生成式AI时代不断涌现的新型伪造手段。

Abstract: The rapid rise of generative AI has made multimodal fake news increasingly realistic and pervasive, posing severe threats to public trust and social stability. Existing detection methods rely heavily on manipulation-specific models and large-scale labeled data, resulting in poor generalization to emerging manipulation types. We observed that the essence of manipulated misinformation lies in its intrinsic conflicts, \textbf{i.e.,} semantic or physical inconsistencies either across modalities or with common world knowledge. Inspired by this observation, we propose \textbf{C}onflict-\textbf{O}riented \textbf{RE}asoning (\textbf{CORE}) framework, an effective paradigm that learns to endows multimodal large language models (MLLMs) with explicit conflict-capturing capability. To this end, CORE first constructs the Conflict Attribution Corpus (CAC) with fine-grained annotations of conflict factors and sources, providing essential data support for subsequent conflict perception training. By performing conflict-oriented representation enhancement and reasoning based on CAC, CORE achieves robust and generalizable conflict detection, effectively and rapidly adapting to unseen manipulation types with a few samples or in even zero-shot settings. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CORE surpasses state-of-the-art models. The dataset and code are publicly available at https://github.com/shen8424/CORE.

</details>


### [94] [DELTAMEM: Incremental Experience Memory for LLM Agents via Residual Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03083)
*Haoran Tan,Zeyu Zhang,Zhicheng Cao,Rui Li,Xu Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DeltaMem 提出一种基于残差树结构的记忆框架，将 LLM 智能体的经验组织为增量式残差存储，通过目标条件技能树和场景知识树消除冗余、缓解检索冲突，并借助自主整合机制实现从通用到专用的自组织。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 智能体将经验存储为扁平独立单元，导致两个核心问题：(1) 相似情节重复存储造成大量冗余；(2) 细微场景变化导致检索到的记忆相互矛盾。本质上，新经验往往是已有知识的增量变化，但现有方法未能利用这一特性。

Method: DeltaMem 构建两棵独立的残差树——目标条件任务经验树（存储可复用技能）和场景级环境知识树。每棵树以根节点存储通用基础经验，增量 delta 节点存储后续变体。检索采用失败惩罚相似度扫描定位最佳匹配，并通过根到匹配节点的链式组合重建完整经验。自主整合机制将高频路径蒸馏为新根节点，使树结构从通用启发式自组织为专用变体。

Result: 在多种交互环境中的实验表明，DeltaMem 一致优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: DeltaMem 通过残差树结构有效解决了经验记忆的冗余和检索冲突问题，其增量式组织与自主整合机制为 LLM 智能体的持续学习提供了高效、可扩展的记忆方案。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based agents increasingly rely on memory to learn from experiences over continual interactions. However, storing experiences as independent, flat units leads to substantial redundancy and retrieval conflicts, as similar episodes repeat overlapping content and subtle scene variations cause retrieved memories to offer contradictory guidance. To address this, we introduce residual experience, positing that newly acquired experience is often an incremental variation of existing knowledge. We propose DeltaMem, a framework that organizes experience memory into two independent residual trees, one storing goal-conditioned task experience as reusable skills and another for scene-level environment knowledge. Each tree uses a root node for generalized base experiences and incremental delta nodes for subsequent variations, allowing related experiences to share a common foundation without duplication. For retrieval, a failure-penalized similarity scan locates the best match, reconstructing the full experience via root-to-match chain composition. An autonomous consolidation mechanism distills high-frequency paths into new root nodes, enabling the trees to self-organize from general heuristics to specialized variants. Experiments across diverse interactive environments show that DeltaMem consistently outperforms existing baselines. To facilitate future research, we release the code at https://github.com/import-myself/DeltaMem.

</details>


### [95] [The Shadow Price of Reasoning: Economic Perspective on Optimal Budget Allocation for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03092)
*Xu Wan,Speed Zhu,Jianwei Cai,Guang Chen,XiMing Huang,Wiggin Zhou,Mingyang Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CLEAR框架，将推理预算分配建模为全局约束优化问题，利用影子价格平衡边际效用，通过"理性放弃"不可解查询并将资源重分配给接近涌现阈值的可解查询，在资源稀缺场景下实现最高3倍精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际部署LLM时存在严格的计算预算约束，而传统方法缺乏对推理时资源分配的全局优化视角，导致资源浪费在无望解决的查询上，未能有效提升整体精度。

Method: 将推理预算分配建模为经济驱动的全局约束优化问题：使用shifted-surge函数对单查询推理效用建模；推导基于全局影子价格的最优分配策略，使得稀缺资源下边际效用均衡；据此实现CLEAR策略——对无法解决的查询进行理性放弃，将资源重新分配给接近涌现阈值的可解查询。

Result: 在多种推理任务及不同流量流上的实验表明，CLEAR显著改善了总token成本与平均精度的Pareto前沿。在资源稀缺场景下，相比均匀分配，CLEAR实现高达3倍的全局精度提升。

Conclusion: 通过经济学原理驱动的全局约束优化视角，CLEAR为推理预算分配提供了理论上有保障、实践上高效的解决方案，推动了推理时缩放在实际部署中的应用。

Abstract: Inference-time scaling has emerged as a critical avenue for enhancing Large Language Models' performance, yet real-world deployment is constrained by strict computational budgets. In this work, we formulate inference budget allocation as a global constrained optimization problem governed by economic principles. By modeling per-query reasoning utility with a shifted-surge function, we derive an optimal allocation policy based on a global shadow price that equilibrates marginal utility under resource scarcity. Based on this theory, we propose Constrained Latent-utility Equilibrium Allocation for Reasoning (CLEAR). It performs rational abandonment and reallocates resources from insolvent queries to solvable queries near their emergence thresholds.
  Extensive experiments on several reasoning tasks with different traffic streams demonstrate that CLEAR significantly improves the Pareto frontier of total token cost versus mean accuracy. In resource-scarce regimes, CLEAR achieves up to a 3x improvement in global accuracy compared to uniform allocation.

</details>


### [96] [Decomposing how prompting steers behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03093)
*Fan L. Cheng,Nikolaus Kriegeskorte*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出嵌套几何分解框架，将提示词对模型内部表征的重塑分解为平移、刚性变换、轴缩放、仿射变换和非线性变换五个层次，发现仿射变换（跨维线性混合）是提示词重组表征以匹配任务结构的关键机制，并在多个LLM和VLM上验证了表征几何与行为恢复的因果关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示词无需更新权重即可引导LLM/VLM行为，但其如何通过改变内部表征几何来实现这一点的机制尚不清晰。现有研究缺乏一个系统性的几何框架来分解和理解提示引起的表征变化。

Method: 提出嵌套几何分解框架：对同一刺激在两种提示下的表征进行对齐，依次施加表达能力递增的刺激不变映射（平移→刚性变换+均匀缩放→序贯轴缩放→仿射变换→非线性变换）。随后进行因果检验：将单层隐藏状态替换为映射后的对应状态，测量目标提示的表征几何与行为恢复程度。通过交叉验证方差分解量化各几何层级的贡献。

Result: 在3个LLM、3个VLM、6个数据集上实验表明：(1) 提示一致地将表征重塑向目标任务结构；(2) 大量激活变化可被形状保持映射（尤其平移和刚性变换）捕获；(3) 仿射变换是首个几乎完全恢复目标提示任务几何并带来相应行为增益的层级，表明跨维线性混合是关键机制；(4) 不同模型和任务呈现差异化的逐层路由策略。

Conclusion: 提示词通过嵌套几何变换层次重塑内部表征，其中仿射变换（跨维线性混合）是实现任务结构重组的关键几何机制。该框架将提示引起的表征变化分解为可解释的几何成分，揭示了模型如何逐层路由任务相关信息以产生提示驱动行为。

Abstract: Prompting steers large language models (LLMs) and vision-language models (VLMs) without weight updates, but it remains unclear how instruction changes reshape internal representations to produce behavior. We introduce a nested geometric decomposition framework that treats prompting as a transformation of the representational geometry of the content following the prompt. For each prompt pair, we align representations of the same stimuli under two prompts using increasingly expressive stimulus-invariant maps: translation, rigid transformation with uniform scaling, sequential axis scaling, affine transformation, and nonlinear transformation. We then causally test each map by replacing a single layer's prompt-A hidden state for held-out stimuli with its mapped counterpart and measuring recovery of prompt-B representational geometry and behavior. Across three LLMs, three VLMs, and six text or image datasets spanning style, emotion, scene content, and number, prompts consistently reshape representations toward the instructed task structure. Cross-validated variance decomposition shows that much prompt-induced activation change is captured by shape-preserving maps, especially translation and rigid transformation with uniform scaling, while tier profiles reveal model- and task-specific routing strategies across layers. Crucially, although translation and rigid tiers already improve behavioral agreement, affine transformation is the first tier to nearly recover target-prompt task geometry and yields corresponding behavioral gains. This suggests that cross-dimensional linear mixing is a key mechanism by which prompts reorganize representations toward instructed task structure. Our framework decomposes prompt-induced representational change into interpretable geometric components and reveals how models route task-relevant structure to produce prompt-driven behavior.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [97] [Position: Prioritize Identifying Structure, Not Complex Models, for Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02632)
*Tyler H. McCormick*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 在高维代理变量场景下，机器学习模型（尤其是LLM）面临机制性欠定问题——多种互不相容的机制能产生相同的观测关系，而LLM倾向于将等价解释类坍缩为单一流畅叙事，这对科学发现构成独特风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM等现代ML模型越来越多地被用于从观测数据中生成科学假设和机制性解释，需警惕一个根本性问题：在ML擅长的高维代理变量领域，机制推断本质上是欠定的。论文旨在揭示这一认识论风险，尤其是LLM在科学发现中的潜在危害。

Method: 本文是一篇立场论文（position paper），通过概念分析和论证，阐述高维代理变量情境下机制学习的欠定性，分析LLM将多种等价解释坍缩为单一叙事的倾向，并提出"机制性机器学习"的具体规范标准。

Result: 核心论证结果包括：(1) 在高维代理变量体系中，预测成功与解释的连贯性不足以作为机制发现的有效证据；(2) LLM会掩盖等价解释的多样性，制造虚假的确定性；(3) 需要建立专门的方法论规范来约束LLM在科学工作流中的使用。

Conclusion: 若LLM中心化的科学工作流要真正服务于科学而非仅模拟科学外观，必须采纳"机制性ML"的严格规范，否则将面临以流畅叙事替代真实机制发现的风险。

Abstract: Modern Machine Learning (ML) and Artificial Intelligence (AI) models, especially large language models (LLMs), are increasingly used to generate scientific hypotheses and mechanistic explanations from observational data. This position paper argues that in the high-dimensional proxy regimes where modern ML excels, mechanistic learning is generically underdetermined: many incompatible mechanisms induce essentially the same observational relationships on the support of the data, so predictive success and coherent explanations are insufficient evidence of mechanism discovery. This underdetermination becomes uniquely hazardous with large language models (LLMs), which tend to collapse large equivalence classes of explanations into a single fluent narrative. This paper proposes concrete standards for ``mechanistic ML,'' and argues these norms are necessary if LLM-centered workflows are to support science rather than merely simulate it.

</details>


### [98] [Target Updates May Stabilize Linear Q-Learning: Periodic and Soft Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02645)
*Donghwan Lee*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文对线性函数近似Q学习中的目标更新机制（周期性硬更新和软更新）进行了严格的理论分析，利用切换线性系统动力学和联合谱半径证明了在显式谱和步长条件下，这些机制能保证收敛到精确的投影Q-Bellman解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周期性目标更新和软目标更新在Q学习和Actor-Critic方法中已被广泛实证验证为有效的稳定化机制，但其精确的理论解释仍不完善。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，为这些经验性机制提供严格的数学基础。

Method: 作者将线性Q学习建模为由Bellman最优算子诱导的精确切换线性系统，利用联合谱半径（JSR）分析切换矩阵族的稳定性。主要分析在确定性线性Q学习框架下展开（目标更新机制最为透明），随后将确定性格态替换为采样随机格态，并加入随机噪声分析，推广到随机强化学习场景。

Result: 证明了在满足显式的谱条件和步长条件下，周期性硬目标更新和软目标更新均能保证线性Q学习收敛到精确的投影Q-Bellman解，即使线性Q学习在一般情况下可能无法收敛。

Conclusion: 通过JSR证书为确定性均值递归建立收敛性后，可自然地推广到随机强化学习场景。该分析为长期以来的经验性目标更新机制提供了严格的理论支撑。

Abstract: Periodic target updates in Q-learning and soft target updates in actor-critic methods are empirically well established stabilization mechanisms, but their precise theoretical explanation is still incomplete. This paper gives a rigorous and exact analysis of these mechanisms for Q-learning with linear function approximation (linear Q-learning) using the exact switched linear system (SLS) dynamics induced by the Bellman maximum and the joint spectral radius (JSR) of the resulting switching matrix families. Although linear Q-learning can fail to converge in general, we prove that, under explicit spectral and step-size conditions, periodic hard target updates and soft target updates can guarantee convergence to the exact projected Q-Bellman solution. The main analysis is carried out for deterministic linear Q-learning, where the target-update mechanism is most transparent. Once the corresponding JSR certificate is established for the mean recursion, the stochastic reinforcement-learning setting can be treated by replacing deterministic modes with sampled stochastic modes and adding the corresponding stochastic-noise analysis.

</details>


### [99] [State-Coupled Volatility in Latent Dynamical Systems: Recovery Under Partial Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02664)
*Imani Beckett*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种状态耦合的随机波动率框架，允许潜过程方差随潜状态位置变化，并通过粒子期望最大化算法在部分观测条件下对其进行估计。仿真结果表明，在强耦合和高观测噪声条件下，该方法相比异方差代理方法具有更低的估计偏差和更强的检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统潜状态空间模型通常假设过程变异性与潜状态位置无关（恒定方差），但在生物、行为和生理系统中，变异性往往依赖于底层动力学状态。这种结构化随机性无法被常方差模型捕捉，因此需要一种能够建模状态依赖波动率的新框架。

Method: 提出状态耦合随机波动率框架，引入耦合参数γ量化潜状态位置与过程变异性的关联强度。估计方法采用粒子期望最大化（EM）算法，结合自助粒子滤波和反向轨迹平滑，以处理部分观测下的推断问题。通过大规模仿真基准测试，系统评估了不同耦合强度、观测噪声水平、轨迹长度和持续性条件下的恢复与检测性能。

Result: 1. 该框架相比基于观测状态的异方差代理方法，始终降低了恢复偏差，在强耦合条件下改善最为显著；2. 恢复性能随潜过程持续性增强而提升；3. 检测性能在广泛条件下保持竞争力，且随观测噪声增大优势更加明显；4. 结果表明，在显式建模潜状态结构时，状态耦合波动率可在部分观测下被有效识别和估计。

Conclusion: 结构化随机性在显式建模时可从部分观测数据中被识别，为研究状态依赖变异性提供了实用的方法论基础，并有助于评估结构化随机性是否携带超出均值状态轨迹的系统动力学信息。

Abstract: Latent state-space models are widely used to study partially observed dynamical systems, yet most formulations assume that process variability is independent of latent-state position. In many biological, behavioral, and physiological systems, however, variability may depend systematically on the underlying dynamical state, producing structured stochasticity that is not captured by constant-variance models. We introduce a state-coupled stochastic volatility framework in which latent process variance depends on displacement from a latent equilibrium. To estimate this relationship under partial observation, we develop a particle expectation-maximization procedure combining bootstrap particle filtering and backward trajectory smoothing. The model includes a coupling parameter, $γ$, that quantifies the strength of association between latent-state position and process variability. A large-scale simulation benchmark evaluated recovery and detection performance across varying coupling strengths, observation noise levels, trajectory lengths, and persistence regimes. The proposed framework consistently reduced recovery bias relative to an observed-state heteroskedastic proxy, with the largest improvements occurring under strong coupling. Recovery performance improved with increasing latent persistence, while detection performance remained competitive across a broad range of conditions and became increasingly advantageous as observation noise increased. Taken together, the results demonstrate that state-coupled volatility can be identified and estimated under partial observation when latent-state structure is explicitly modeled. The framework provides a practical methodological foundation for studying state-dependent variability and evaluating whether structured stochasticity contributes information about system dynamics beyond that contained in mean-state trajectories alone.

</details>


### [100] [ScoreStop: Gradient-based early stopping using functional score tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02740)
*Oliver J. Hines,Christian L. Hines*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: ScoreStop 提出了一种基于梯度的早停规则，将每次迭代的停止决策转化为对原假设（当前预测器为总体风险最小化器）的检验，利用在验证集上计算的函数得分检验，其统计量在更新方向上尺度不变且在原假设下具有已知的渐近分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有梯度提升决策树的早停规则依赖验证损失和固定的耐心（patience）参数，但耐心参数缺乏可解释的尺度，且验证损失可能带有噪声或由用户隐式定义（如 LambdaRank 的隐式损失）。需要一种更具普适性、可解释性且不依赖显式损失值的早停方法。

Method: 提出 ScoreStop：在每次迭代中，将"当前预测器是否为总体风险最小化器"作为原假设，在验证集上构造一个函数得分检验（functional score test）。该检验的统计量在梯度更新方向上具有尺度不变性，且在原假设下具有已知的渐近分布。由于使用梯度而非损失值，该方法可直接适用于隐式损失（如 LambdaRank）以及通过影响函数处理的数据依赖损失（如 Cox 回归）。

Result: 在合成实验和真实数据基准测试中，ScoreStop 表现出与基于损失值的早停方法相当的竞争力。

Conclusion: ScoreStop 为梯度提升决策树提供了一种理论上可靠、适用范围更广的早停方案，尤其在处理隐式损失和数据依赖损失时具有独特优势。

Abstract: Gradient boosted decision trees require a stopping rule to avoid overfitting. The standard rule monitors a validation loss and stops if the loss fails to improve for a fixed patience period. However, the patience parameter has no interpretable scale and validation losses can be noisy or implicitly defined by a user-specified gradient. We propose ScoreStop, a gradient-based early-stopping rule that casts the stopping decision at each iteration as a test of the null hypothesis that the current predictor is the population risk minimizer. We use a functional score test, computed on validation data, with a statistic that is scale-invariant in the update direction, with a known asymptotic distribution under the null. Because our test uses gradients rather than loss values, the same construction applies to implicit losses such as LambdaRank, and data-dependent losses such as Cox regression via influence functions. In synthetic experiments and real-data benchmarks, we show that ScoreStop is competitive with loss-based methods.

</details>


### [101] [Scalable Derivative Gaussian Processes via Exact Gradient Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02909)
*Hyunseok Seung,Matthias Katzfuss*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: TERA提出了一种基于目标特定精确梯度约简的高斯过程方法，通过证明平稳核函数下梯度分量与目标值的条件独立性，将条件密度仅用最多m²个方向导数表征，结合Vecchia近似将计算复杂度从O(n³d³)降至O(dm²+m⁶)，实现了与维度d近乎无关的高可扩展推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度观测能显著改善GP代理模型，尤其在高维昂贵函数评估场景中。但精确推断需联合处理n个函数值和n个完整梯度，计算复杂度为O(n³d³)，在高维情况下完全不可行。亟需一种能同时解耦样本量n和维度d的可扩展方法。

Method: 作者提出TERA（Target-specific Exact gradient Reduction Approximation）：(1) 从理论上证明对于平稳核函数，与连接目标点和条件点的方向正交的梯度分量，在给定条件集后与目标函数值条件独立；(2) 因此精确条件密度仅需至多m²个方向导数即可完全表征（m为条件集大小）；(3) 将这些降维的、与维度无关的条件密度作为Vecchia近似中的局部因子，从而将稠密矩阵求逆与n和d解耦。

Result: TERA将每次目标评估成本降至O(dm²+m⁶)时间和O(dm²+m⁴)内存，且底层导数GP模型在数学上保持不变。实验表明TERA达到最先进预测精度，同时比标准导数GP快数个数量级，且计算时间和GPU内存峰值随维度d基本保持平稳。

Conclusion: TERA通过理论驱动的梯度降维与Vecchia近似结合，成功打破了导数GP的维度灾难，实现了高维空间中精度无损且高度可扩展的推断，为昂贵高维优化等应用提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Gradient observations can substantially improve Gaussian process (GP) surrogates, particularly in high-dimensional settings where function evaluations are expensive. However, exact inference with $n$ function values and $n$ full gradients in $d$ dimensions scales cubically in the joint state size, imposing an intractable $\mathcal{O}(n^3 d^3)$ computational bottleneck. We introduce TERA, a highly scalable derivative GP method based on target-specific exact gradient reduction. We prove that for stationary kernels, the gradient components orthogonal to the directions connecting the target and conditioning points are conditionally independent of the target function value; consequently, the exact conditional density is fully characterized by at most $m^2$ directional derivatives once a conditioning set of size $m$ is specified. By using these reduced, dimension-free conditionals as local factors in a Vecchia approximation, TERA effectively decouples $n$ and $d$ from the dense matrix inversion. This reduces the per-target evaluation cost to $\mathcal{O}(dm^2 + m^6)$ time and $\mathcal{O}(dm^2 + m^4)$ memory, leaving the underlying derivative GP model mathematically unchanged. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that TERA achieves state-of-the-art predictive accuracy while operating orders of magnitude faster than standard derivative GPs. Crucially, both computation time and peak GPU memory remain essentially flat with respect to $d$, enabling highly scalable inference in high-dimensional spaces.

</details>


### [102] [Trajectory-Aware Node Contributions and the Limits of Static Controllability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03067)
*Valentina Kuskova,Dmitry Zaytsev,Michael Coppedge*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文定义了“涌现贡献（EC）”这一有限时域节点动态杠杆度量，它是沿系统轨迹累积的脉冲响应度量加权能量。EC 基于任何可微模型的雅可比矩阵计算，在线性时不变极限下退化为平均可控性。作者通过受控合成数据刻画了两者一致与分歧的条件，并将五种真实系统置于该相空间中，揭示了 EC 在持续机制切换等场景下提供超越静态可控性的信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖静态图中心性或线性时不变假设下的可控性格拉姆矩阵，而实际估计系统通常是非线性且时变的，需要一种不依赖线性假设、适用于非线性时变系统的节点贡献度量方法。

Method: 定义 EC 为有限时域内节点脉冲响应沿系统轨迹的度量加权能量，基于任意可微模型的雅可比矩阵计算；使用已知真实贡献的受控合成数据族，在非线性程度、机制结构、持续性和扰动幅度四个维度上构建相图；将五种真实估计系统放置在相空间中作为诊断工具，并对其中一个面板进行 20 种子重训练集成的鲁棒性分析。

Result: EC 与平均可控性在静态或平滑漂移动态下一致且均追踪真实贡献；分歧在持续机制切换下出现，在持续符号反转下最强，移除符号反转后分歧消失；极端扰动幅度下两者均退化；真实系统在相空间中的位置可诊断 EC 何时提供超越静态可控性的信息；20 种子重训练集成揭示了稳健的方差-杠杆解耦现象，即节点扰动广泛传播但系统内方差低，静态中心性和方差汇总均无法捕捉该现象。

Conclusion: EC 是一种估计器无关的节点动态贡献度量，在线性时不变极限下精确退化为平均可控性；两者在非线性、时变条件下的分歧可通过相图系统化诊断；EC 在持续机制切换等复杂动态场景下提供了静态方法无法捕捉的信息，值得其额外的计算成本。

Abstract: A recurring data mining task in complex networks is to determine how individual nodes contribute to system behavior. Existing approaches rely on either static-graph centralities or control-theoretic quantities such as controllability Gramians, which assume linear, time-invariant dynamics. Estimated systems, however, are typically nonlinear and time-varying. We define "emergent contribution (EC)," a finite-horizon measure of a node's dynamical leverage: the metric-weighted energy of its impulse response accumulated along the system trajectory. Computed from the Jacobians of any differentiable model, EC is estimator-agnostic and reduces exactly to average controllability in the linear, time-invariant limit. Our contribution is a characterization of when the two measures agree and diverge. Using a controlled synthetic family with known ground-truth contribution, we construct a phase diagram spanning nonlinearity, regime structure, persistence, and perturbation amplitude. EC and average controllability agree under static or smoothly drifting dynamics and both track ground truth. Divergence emerges under persistent regime switching, is strongest under persistent sign reversal, and disappears when the sign reversal is removed. At extreme perturbation amplitudes, both measures degrade, identifying the limits of local linearization. We place five estimated real systems from several domains within this phase space. Their placement serves as a diagnostic of when EC provides information beyond static controllability and therefore justifies its additional computational cost. On one panel examined in depth, a twenty-seed retraining ensemble reveals a robust variance--leverage dissociation: nodes whose perturbations propagate widely despite low within-system variance, which is not recovered by static centralities nor variance-based summaries.

</details>


### [103] [An Asymptotic Theory of Chain-of-Thought in In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03217)
*Kaito Takanami,Cengiz Pehlevan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文在高维渐近框架下，利用随机矩阵理论推导出CoT推理深度与泛化误差的精确公式，揭示了从指数/多项式改进到饱和与过度思考的尖锐相变，并刻画了最优推理深度的缩放规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管思维链（CoT）推理已被广泛用于激发大语言模型的多步推理能力，但CoT深度如何影响泛化性能的缩放行为仍缺乏理论理解。

Method: 作者构建了一个可理论求解的CoT模型——线性回归中的上下文权重预测，将测试时推理建模为权重参数估计的迭代精炼过程。在高维渐近极限下，运用随机矩阵理论推导出泛化误差关于推理深度、预训练数据量和上下文长度的精确解析公式。

Result: 分析揭示了泛化性能随推理深度呈现尖锐相变：依次经历指数级改进、多项式改进、饱和及过度思考（overthinking）阶段。更深推理仅在预训练充分且上下文信息丰富时才有效，否则会导致误差放大或饱和。最优推理深度存在明确的缩放规律，并在全学习的线性注意力和softmax注意力模型上得到实验验证。

Conclusion: 本文为测试时CoT深度如何影响泛化提供了统一的理论解释：推理深度的有效性高度依赖于预训练数据量与上下文信息量，存在由相变边界刻画的最优深度，超出该深度反而有害。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning has become a widely used mechanism for eliciting multi-step reasoning in large language models by generating intermediate reasoning steps at inference time. Yet the scaling behavior of generalization with CoT depth remains poorly understood. To address this question, we study a theoretically solvable model of CoT for in-context weight prediction in linear regression, where test-time reasoning is represented as an iterative refinement of the weight-parameter estimate. Using tools from random matrix theory under high-dimensional asymptotics, we derive an exact formula for the generalization error as a function of reasoning depth, pretraining data amount, and context length. Our analysis reveals a sharp phase transition separating exponential and polynomial improvement, saturation, and overthinking, and characterizes how the optimal reasoning depth scales. We further show that deeper reasoning is most effective with sufficiently rich pretraining and in-context information, whereas limited pretraining or context makes longer reasoning prone to error amplification or saturation. We also validate these predictions through experiments on fully learned linear attention and softmax attention models. Our results provide a unified theoretical account of how test-time CoT depth affects generalization.

</details>


### [104] [Hierarchies of Calibration: Classification meets Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03245)
*Johannes Resin,Lu Yang,Tilmann Gneiting*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理、拓展并桥接了分类与回归任务中的概率校准概念，重点关注各类校准定义之间的层级关系，并提出了名义结果的模态校准、双重PIT校准的独立性等新贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率预测的校准概念在文献中零散分布、定义繁多且关系不清，缺乏统一的框架来理解分类与回归场景下各类校准定义之间的层级结构与逻辑关联。

Method: 通过文献综述与理论分析，系统梳理了实值数据、连续结果、计数数据、名义类别和二元结果下的校准概念；引入模态校准、区分完全/部分/平均校准；证明双重PIT校准与现有离散结果校准概念的逻辑独立性；将基于预测分布泛函（均值、分位数、事件概率）的校准结果进行推广。

Result: 建立了各类校准概念之间的层级关系图谱；证明了双重PIT校准与先前离散校准概念逻辑独立；推广了基于预测分布泛函的校准理论结果；提供了构建示例与反例的算法工具。

Conclusion: 校准概念之间存在严格的层级关系，本文的统一框架有助于研究者更清晰地理解和选择合适的校准定义，模态校准和双重PIT校准的独立性为后续研究提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Concepts of calibration formalize the compatibility between probabilistic predictions and the respective outcomes. In a nutshell, the outcomes ought to be indistinguishable from random draws from the predictive distributions. In this paper, we review, extend, and bridge notions of calibration that have been proposed for classification and regression tasks. Particular emphasis is given to hierarchical relations between the various notions, as they apply to general real-valued data, continuous outcomes, count data, nominal classes, and binary outcomes. To highlight a number of contributions, we introduce the notion of modal calibration for nominal outcomes, we distinguish full, partial, and average calibration in this setting, and we show that double probability integral transform (PIT) calibration is logically independent of previously proposed concepts of calibration for discrete outcomes. Furthermore, we generalize extant results on concepts of calibration that are expressed in terms of properties or functionals of the predictive distributions, such as means, quantiles, or event probabilities. Throughout the paper, we illustrate the concepts and their hierarchical relations in worked examples, and we provide algorithmic tools that support the construction of instructive examples and counterexamples.

</details>


### [105] [Combining Statistical Features and Deep Encodings for Rehearsal-Based Class-Incremental Time Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03292)
*Pablo García-Santaclara,Bruno Fernández-Castro,Rebeca Pilar Díaz-Redondo*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一种基于双流特征提取管道（预训练冻结基础模型的深度时序嵌入 + 统计特征）的类增量持续学习方法，用于多变量时间序列分类，在五个基准数据集上取得竞争性平均精度和低遗忘率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的多变量时间序列分类系统需要不断添加新类别并整合新信息，同时避免灾难性遗忘。现有类增量持续学习在时间序列领域面临数据时序结构带来的额外挑战，需要一种能有效平衡稳定性与可塑性的方法。

Method: 构建双流特征提取管道：(1) 利用预训练冻结的基础模型提取深度时序嵌入特征；(2) 应用统计特征提取。两条流融合后用于类增量持续学习场景下的多变量时间序列分类。

Result: 在五个基准数据集上评估，所提系统在所有数据集上均取得竞争性的平均准确率，同时在所有实验配置下保持较低的遗忘率。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了多变量时间序列的类增量持续学习问题，通过双流架构在保持旧知识（低遗忘）与学习新类别（高精度）之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Many systems used in real-world environments require adding new categories and incorporating new information without forgetting what was previously learnt by the classification model. This is known as class-incremental continual learning, and in the case of multivariate time-series, is further complicated by the temporal structure of the data. In this paper, we present a novel approach for performing class incremental continual learning for the classification of multivariate time series data based upon the construction of a dual-stream feature extraction pipeline (using both deep temporal embedding features generated via a pre-trained frozen foundation model and application of statistical features). Evaluated on five benchmark datasets, the proposed system achieves competitive average accuracy across all datasets while maintaining low forgetting rates across all experimental configurations.

</details>


### [106] [A Robust Optimization Approach to Sparse Principal Component Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03553)
*David Vävinggren,Francis Bach,André M. H. Teixeira,Dave Zachariah,Antônio H. Ribeiro*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文提出对抗性PCA (AdvPCA)，通过鲁棒优化对抗潜在空间的最坏情况扰动来实现稀疏性，避免了传统ℓ₁惩罚项调参困难。该方法可闭式归约为交替优化算法，并具有数据自适应参数化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PCA产生稠密表示，不适用于高维数据。现有稀疏PCA方法依赖ℓ₁惩罚项，但在无监督场景下难以调参。需要一种无需显式稀疏正则化即可获得稀疏解的新方法。

Method: 将PCA重构目标重新表述为鲁棒优化问题：优化编码器和解码器，使其在潜在空间受约束的最坏情况扰动下仍能最小化重构误差。该问题可闭式归约，导出交替迭代算法——稀疏编码器采用对抗性线性回归式更新，解码器采用正交更新。通过理论刻画解的性质，推导出数据自适应参数化策略。

Result: 在合成数据和真实基因组学数据上的数值实验验证了AdvPCA的有效性，表明该方法能够开箱即用地实现稀疏降维。

Conclusion: AdvPCA通过对抗鲁棒优化框架实现了无需显式调参的稀疏PCA，兼具理论保证和实用性能，为高维数据降维提供了新途径。

Abstract: While principal component analysis (PCA) is a fundamental tool for dimensionality reduction, its dense representations make it ill-suited for high-dimensional data. Existing methods address this by promoting sparsity through explicit $\ell_1$-penalties, but these are not obvious to tune due to the unsupervised nature of the task. In contrast, we propose Adversarial PCA (AdvPCA), which leverages robust optimization to achieve sparsity by optimizing the reconstruction objective against bounded, worst-case latent space perturbations. We show that this formulation admits a closed-form reduction, leading to a practical iterative algorithm that alternates between adversarial linear regression-style updates for the sparse encoder and orthogonal updates for the decoder. By theoretically characterizing the solution, we derive a data-adaptive parameterization that allows the algorithm to perform effectively out of the box. We validate these claims through numerical experiments on synthetic and real-world genomics data.

</details>


### [107] [Few-Shot Prediction for Pulsar Noise with Long Short-Term Memory Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03574)
*Qingye Tang,Dechao An,Haoran Peng,Yuqi Ouyang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于MAML优化的LSTM网络，用于在数据稀缺条件下预测脉冲星计时残差，结合粒子群优化自动调参，在IPTA第二版数据上仅需10%微调数据即可实现跨频率子群的鲁棒泛化，且模型轻量高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲星计时阵列（PTA）中毫秒脉冲星不同自旋频率子群普遍存在数据稀缺问题，传统深度学习方法难以在仅有少量真实计时残差的情况下快速适应新的频率域，亟需一种具备少样本泛化能力的预测方案。

Method: 采用模型无关元学习（MAML）算法对长短期记忆（LSTM）网络进行元训练，使其获得良好的初始参数，可在新频率域仅用少量（10%）真实计时残差快速微调适应；同时引入粒子群优化（PSO）算法自动搜索最优超参数，提升预测精度。

Result: 在IPTA第二次数据发布上评估，模型在多个高频测试频率域上三项指标均表现准确，且仅需目标域10%的计时残差进行微调；模型仅占用16.86 MB CPU内存，单步残差预测耗时18毫秒。

Conclusion: 该方案在数据稀疏场景下实现了高效、实时、轻量的脉冲星计时残差预测，特别适用于计算资源受限的实际部署环境，为PTA数据处理提供了实用化解决路径。

Abstract: This work proposes a novel solution to predict pulsar timing residuals with limited data, addressing the critical challenge of data scarcity across spin-frequency subgroups of millisecond pulsars in PTA datasets. The proposed solution applies a Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) network optimized using the model-agnostic meta-learning algorithm, enabling rapid adaptation to new frequency domain by fine-tuning the LSTM network with only a few-shot of ground truth timing residuals. Particle swarm optimization algorithm is also used for automatic hyperparameter optimization, leading to improved prediction accuracy. Our solution, evaluated on the second data release of the International Pulsar Timing Array (IPTA), demonstrates robust generalization with accurate predictions in three metrics across high-frequency test frequency domains, while requiring only 10% of the timing residuals from these domains for model fine-tuning. Furthermore, our lightweight structure only costs 16.86 MB CPU memory and 18 milliseconds for single-step residual prediction. All these characteristics make our solution highly suitable for real-world applications, where effective and real-time predictions of pulsar timing residuals are essential-particularly in resource-constrained environments with limited computational power, memory, or energy availability.

</details>


### [108] [Set-Preserving Calibration from Conformal P-Values to E-Values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03600)

Abstract: Standard conformal prediction (CP) procedures are typically formulated in terms of p-values, but reliance on p-values alone limits flexibility, for example, when combining dependent evidence across models or data splits. Recent work has explored e-value formulations for conformal inference, yet a direct connection between p- and e-value formulations in CP has been missing, especially regarding their statistical efficiency. We first identify limitations of classical p-to-e calibrators in the CP setting, showing that they are not set-preserving and can lead to overly conservative prediction sets. To address this, we propose a novel P2E calibrator that converts conformal p-values into e-values without altering the prediction set induced by the original conformal p-value. We establish both theoretically and empirically that our calibrator can yield significant efficiency gains over existing p-to-e calibrators. This e-value formulation enables principled use of recent advances in e-value merging and randomization, where we demonstrate its impact in two applications: cross-conformal prediction (CCP), whose variants typically provide only approximate $1-2α$ coverage, and conformal aggregation (CA). In both cases, our e-value-based methods satisfy the desired $1-α$ coverage guarantee while improving efficiency over standard baselines. More broadly, our approach expands the flexibility of CP and opens new directions for efficient, distribution-free uncertainty quantification.

</details>


### [109] [Resource-Constrained Adaptive Inference for Sequential Pricing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03736)
*Ruicheng Ao,Jiashuo Jiang,David Simchi-Levi*

Abstract: Resource-constrained pricing controllers can make fixed-price inference impossible: the controller's resource state may remove the target price neighborhood from the feasible set, even when every realized action has a known positive density. We formalize this support-exclusion failure through a local non-identification result and a realized information clock. We then design a target-aware pricing controller that certifies feasible target bands and logs continuous local densities. Localized debiasing gives studentized intervals whose width is governed by this clock. The resulting regret--information accounting, stated up to pilot re-solving error, shows that cheap exploration can be insufficient for inference: polynomial target mass gives polynomial rates, while a pure $1/t$ target branch does not yield shrinking fixed-target intervals without additional local movement. Experiments show calibration in certified bands and diagnostic abstention when the resource state collapses target support.

</details>


### [110] [A Quantitative Approximation Framework for Flow Distillation in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03820)
*Weiguo Gao,Ming Li,Lei Shi,Hanfei Zhou*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: Summary generation failed

Abstract: We develop a quantitative approximation framework for diffusion distillation, viewing few-step sampling as error propagation under compositions of learned flow maps. Focusing on trajectory distillation for the probability-flow ODE, we show that local approximation errors can be strongly amplified in low-noise multimodal regimes, where the underlying dynamics become stiff. In an analytically tractable Gaussian-mixture Ornstein--Uhlenbeck setting, we separate two core difficulties: approximating the time-dependent score field and controlling the dynamical amplification governed by the time-integrated Jacobian bound of the probability-flow ODE. On the approximation side, we prove constructive L^p(p_t) guarantees showing that ReLU--ReQU networks approximate the Gaussian-mixture score uniformly over time, with depth and width scaling polylogarithmically in the target accuracy and explicitly with the mixture geometry. On the stability side, we derive an explicit bound L(t) for the spatial Lipschitz constant of the probability-flow velocity and convert it into a flow map stability estimate governed by \int_s^t L(u)\,du, making late-time amplification in stiff regimes computable. Building on these estimates, we prove that deep residual compositions efficiently approximate the long-horizon transport, with global error controlled by the stability amplification factor, and identify a Lipschitz-mismatch regime in which one-step distillation is structurally unfavorable. The resulting theory yields a stability-balanced non-uniform time grid obtained by uniform partitioning in the cumulative stability coordinate. Experiments support the prediction and reduce end-to-end relative MSE by up to 51.9\% with 8 segments compared with uniform grids.

</details>


### [111] [Privacy-Robust Incrementality Measurement for Advertising Systems under Signal Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03878)
*Prashant Shekhar,Caroline Howard*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>

Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Advertising platforms use randomized lift tests to measure incrementality, but privacy-preserving reporting systems degrade the observed signal through match-rate loss, linkability loss, attribution-window loss, aggregation-threshold suppression, randomized reporting noise, and segment-heterogeneous signal loss. This paper formulates privacy-constrained advertising measurement as a robust causal decision problem under the mentioned signal losses. Given a randomized experiment and an ambiguity set for privacy-induced degradation, the framework projects the observation-compatible fiber of clean/unfiltered experimental worlds onto the incrementality functional and returns certified, rejected, and unresolved decisions. The main result gives a sharp decision frontier. Reports outside the frontier support uniformly valid certification or rejection, whereas reports inside it contain too little information for any method to uniformly distinguish above-threshold incrementality from non-incrementality. Supporting results give finite-sample certification, sample-complexity guarantees, a minimax lower bound showing that signal loss reduces effective information, and a reporting-granularity tradeoff. On 2.0M Criteo Uplift rows and the 64K-row Hillstrom email experiment, clean conversion lift is positive in both datasets, with lifts 0.00112 and 0.00495, respectively. Population certification survives mild degradation in Criteo and severe degradation in Hillstrom, while all considered finite-sample stress settings in both datasets remain unresolved after simultaneous uncertainty and reporting noise are included. Overall, the research contributes a decision-theoretic layer for privacy-aware incrementality measurement whose output is the strongest causal-claim justified by degraded ads signals.

</details>
